## Evidence Standards Used

- Highest weight: dated MCP bytes, dword values, function bounds, xrefs, disassembly/decompilation, stack/prototype/comment prestates, RTTI names, and decoded COL/CHD/BCD records, accepted only with the supervisor's dynamic session-health and canonical-disk identity reread at each Gate 2B/current-state/post-move check.
- Strong corroboration: current exact by-memory files, current Singleton template authority, generated source state, current coverage rows, and startup/shutdown control flow.
- Heuristic layer: normal MSVC x86 scalar-deleting-destructor lowering, EBO, null-preserving base-to-derived conversion, explicit template specialization placement, and mid-2000s header/source organization.
- Historical reports are leads only. The old B003 split evidence is preserved, while its manual singleton assignments and UID allocations are superseded by current proof.
- Negative evidence is retained: zero direct destructor-body callers, absent original sanitizer symbol, absent local TextFilter UDT, and no source-authored raw table route.

Fresh sanitizer child CPP: preserve the current complete human body while restoring the proven `__stdcall` calling convention:

```cpp
void __stdcall SanitizeWideTextForChat(wchar_t *text, unsigned int textLength)
{
    for (unsigned int i = 0; i < textLength; ++i) {
        wchar_t ch = text[i];
        if (ch == 0)
            break;

        if (ch == L'\n' || ch == L'\r' || (ch >= L' ' && ch <= 0x7f))
            continue;

        switch (ch) {
        case 0x00e9:
        case 0x00e8:
        case 0x00e0:
        case 0x00f9:
        case 0x00f4:
        case 0x00ee:
        case 0x00eb:
        case 0x00ef:
        case 0x00fb:
        case 0x00fa:
        case 0x00e2:
        case 0x00e7:
            continue;
        default:
            text[i] = L'?';
            break;
        }
    }
}
```
